\displaystyle\leq C\left(\varepsilon^{6}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{7}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{5}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[g_{3}(h_{1})\nabla_{\varepsilon}(h_{1}-h_{2})\right]\|_{L^{2}}

(5.49)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\varepsilon^{6}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{7}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{5}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

For the high-order derivative term, Leibniz rule yields:

(5.50)

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}\left[g_{3}(h_{1})\nabla_{\varepsilon}(h_{1}-h_{2})\right]\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\leq\sum_{m=0}^{3}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{m}g_{3}(h_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{6-m}(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+\sum_{m=4}^{5}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{m}g_{3}(h_{1})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{6-m}(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\leq C\varepsilon^{5}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.\end{split}

Combining (
5.48
), (
5.49
), and (
5.50
):

(5.51)

\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[g_{3}(h_{1})\nabla_{\varepsilon}(h_{1}-h_{2})\right]\|_{\mathcal{F}_{5}}\leq C\varepsilon^{5}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

Estimation of the second term in (
5.46
).

Similarly, we have

(5.52)

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{\mathcal{F}_{5}}\\
&\leq C\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\quad+C\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+C\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{2}}.\end{split}

The uniform bounds yield:

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{\infty}}

(5.53)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\varepsilon^{7}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{8}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{6}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{2}}

(5.54)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{8}\left(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{6}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

For the high-order derivative term, note that
g_{3}(h)
depends on
|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}
:

(5.55)

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\leq C\varepsilon^{8}\sum_{m=1}^{3}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{m}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{6-m}(|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}|^{2}-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+C\varepsilon^{8}\sum_{m=4}^{6}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{m}h_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{6-m}(|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}|^{2}-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}|^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

The required derivative bounds are:

(5.56)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C,

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{3}{2}},

(5.57)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{4}h_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C,

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}h_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-1},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{6}h_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-2},

and

(5.58)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{m}(|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}|^{2}-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}|^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{2m+1}{2}}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},\quad m=0,1,2,

(5.59)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}(|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}|^{2}-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},

(5.60)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}(|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}|^{2}-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-3}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

Combining (
5.56
)–(
5.60
) into (
5.55
):

(5.61)

\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{4}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

Synthesizing (
5.53
), (
5.54
), and (
5.61
):

(5.62)

\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[(g_{3}(h_{1})-g_{3}(h_{2}))\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{\mathcal{F}_{5}}\leq C\varepsilon^{4}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

Conclusion.

Substituting (
5.51
) and (
5.62
) into (
5.46
), we obtain the final estimate for the divergence term:

(5.63)

\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[g_{3}(h_{1})\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{1}-g_{3}(h_{2})\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\right]\|_{\mathcal{F}_{5}}\leq C\varepsilon^{4}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}.

This completes the proof of the required estimates for all components of
\mathfrak{h}_{\varepsilon,\phi_{1}}-\mathfrak{h}_{\varepsilon,\phi_{2}}
.
∎

Now we are able to give the proof of Theorem
1.1
.

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

With Lemmas
5.2
and
5.3
, we have established that for a given function
\phi
, a solution to (
5.2
) exists. Consequently, the original problem is equivalent to finding a solution to (
5.1
). We rewrite (
5.1
) as

(5.64)

\begin{split}\mathcal{L}_{\varepsilon}\phi&=\hat{P}_{1}+\hat{P}_{2}+P_{3}+\hat{P}_{4},\end{split}

where

\displaystyle\hat{P}_{1}

\displaystyle:=P_{1}+\frac{3}{2c}\partial_{1}\bigl((\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}\bigr)-\left(2A+\tfrac{1}{3}\right)\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{2}^{2}q,

\displaystyle\hat{P}_{2}

\displaystyle:=P_{2}-\sigma(1+\varepsilon^{2})\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{2}^{4}q,

\displaystyle\hat{P}_{4}

\displaystyle:=P_{4}-\mathcal{L}_{2}q.

We shall find a solution to (
5.64
) using a fixed-point argument.

For any
\phi\in\mathcal{F}_{\phi}
, we first establish the estimate

(5.65)

\big\|\partial_{1}^{-1}\hat{P}_{1}\big\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon.

To prove this, we verify that all terms in
\partial_{1}^{-1}\hat{P}_{1}
are bounded by
C\varepsilon
in the
\|\cdot\|_{**}
norm. We illustrate the process with
(\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}
and
\varepsilon^{2}\mathcal{Q}\Pi
, as representative cases; the remaining terms can be handled similarly, and we omit the details for brevity.

For
(\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}
, we compute

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\phi\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}((\partial_{1}\phi)^{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{1}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\phi\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{8/3}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{2}}^{3/2}\|\partial_{1}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{1/2}\leq C\|\phi\|_{*}^{3/2}.

This yields

\|(\partial_{1}\phi)^{2}\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon.

For
\varepsilon^{2}\mathcal{Q}\Pi=\varepsilon^{2}\Pi+\frac{1}{3}\varepsilon^{4}\mathcal{P}\Pi
, where

\displaystyle\Pi

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{2}f)^{2}+\frac{1}{2(1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2})}(-c\partial_{1}h+\varepsilon^{2}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h\cdot\nabla_{\varepsilon}f)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma\varepsilon^{4}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[\frac{-|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}}(1+\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}})}\right],

we begin with
(\partial_{2}f)^{2}
:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|(\partial_{2}f)^{2}\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}(\|(\partial_{2}q)^{2}\|_{**}+\|(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**})

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}(1+\|(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**}),

where

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}((\partial_{2}\phi)^{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\phi\|_{*}\leq C\varepsilon.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{4}\|\mathcal{P}(\partial_{2}f)^{2}\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4}(\|\mathcal{P}(\partial_{2}q)^{2}\|_{**}+\|\mathcal{P}(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**})

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4}(1+\|\mathcal{P}(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**}),

with

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{4}\|\mathcal{P}(\partial_{2}\phi)^{2}\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|\phi\|_{*}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}\phi\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\phi\|_{L^{4}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}.

Next, consider
\varepsilon^{2}g_{4}(h)g_{2}(h,f)
and
\varepsilon^{2}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}(h)\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]
, where

\displaystyle g_{2}(h,f)

\displaystyle=(-c\partial_{1}h+\varepsilon^{2}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h\cdot\nabla_{\varepsilon}f)^{2},

\displaystyle g_{4}(h)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2(1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2})},

\displaystyle g_{5}(h)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma\varepsilon^{4}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}}{\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}}(1+\sqrt{1+\varepsilon^{6}|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h|^{2}})}.

We obtain

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|g_{4}(h)g_{2}(h,f)\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|g_{4}g_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}(g_{4}g_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|g_{4}g_{2}\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon,

and

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}.

For the higher-order terms:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{4}\|\mathcal{P}(g_{4}g_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{4}\left(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(g_{4}g_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(g_{4}g_{2})\|_{L^{4/3}}+\|\partial_{1}\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(g_{4}g_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{4}\|\mathcal{P}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{4}\left(\|\mathcal{P}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{P}\nabla_{\varepsilon}\cdot[g_{5}\nabla_{\varepsilon}h]\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}.

Combining these estimates yields

\|\varepsilon^{2}\mathcal{Q}\Pi\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon.

Next, we estimate
\|\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon
using representative terms
\varepsilon^{4}\mathcal{P}(h\partial_{2}f)
and
\varepsilon^{2}h\partial_{2}f
from
\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}
. We compute:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{4}\|\mathcal{P}(h\partial_{2}f)\|_{***}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{4}\left(\|\mathcal{P}(h\partial_{2}f)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{2}\mathcal{P}(h\partial_{2}f)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{P}(h\partial_{2}f)\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|h\partial_{2}f\|_{***}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\left(\|h\partial_{2}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{2}(h\partial_{2}f)\|_{L^{2}}+\|h\partial_{2}f\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon.

This establishes the bound
\|\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon
.

For
P_{3}
, we recall the operator representation
|D|=-\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\partial_{x_{1}}-\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)\partial_{x_{2}}
, where
D=(-i\partial_{x_{1}},-i\partial_{x_{2}})
and the Riesz transforms
\mathscr{R}_{j}(D)=i\frac{D_{j}}{|D|}
are bounded on
W^{s,q}
for
1<q<\infty
and
s\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. The expression for
P_{3}
is:

\displaystyle P_{3}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\varepsilon^{-4}}{c^{2}}G_{2}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f-\frac{\varepsilon^{-4}}{c^{2}}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f-\frac{\varepsilon^{-2}}{c^{2}}\left(\sigma+\tfrac{1}{3}\right)\mathcal{P}G_{2}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\varepsilon^{-2}}{c^{2}}\left(\sigma+\tfrac{1}{3}\right)\mathcal{P}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f-\frac{1}{3c^{2}}\sigma\mathcal{P}^{2}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f.

To establish the estimates
\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon
and
\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon
, we analyze representative terms:
\varepsilon^{-4}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f
,
\varepsilon^{-2}\mathcal{P}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f
, and
\mathcal{P}^{2}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f
.

Term 1:

\varepsilon^{-4}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f=-\varepsilon^{-4}\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi-\varepsilon^{-3}\partial_{2}\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi

Using Theorem
3.5
and Remark
3.1
, we estimate:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-4}\left(\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}\cdot\|_{L^{2}}+\|\cdot\|_{L^{4/3}}\right)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-5/2}\||D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{1}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\cdot\|_{L^{4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/4}\||D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\eta\|_{C^{1}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}.

By Theorem
3.6
, we have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\partial_{x_{1}}|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{1}}^{2}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{1}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}.

Combining these yields:

\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon^{3}.

Similarly,
\varepsilon^{-3}\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon^{3}
.

Term 2:

\varepsilon^{-2}\mathcal{P}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f=-\varepsilon^{-2}\partial_{1}^{2}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f-\partial_{2}^{2}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f

Theorem
3.6
provides:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{1}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-2}\left(\|\partial_{1}R_{3}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}R_{3}\|_{L^{4/3}}+\|\partial_{1}^{2}R_{3}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{1}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|\partial_{x_{1}}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{-5/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{2}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{1}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\partial_{x_{1}}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{W^{1,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\eta\|_{C^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\eta\|_{C^{2}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{4},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{1}^{2}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|R_{3}(\eta)\xi\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{3}}^{2}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{-7/2}\|\eta\|_{C^{3}}^{2}\|\eta\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}.

Thus,

\|\varepsilon^{-2}\partial_{1}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon^{3}.

Similarly,
\|\partial_{2}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon^{3}
.

Term 3:

\mathcal{P}^{2}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f=-\partial_{1}^{2}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{2}^{2}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f

We compute:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{2}\|\partial_{1}^{m}\mathcal{P}R_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}R_{1}\|_{L^{4/3}}.

From (
2.4
):

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}(-c\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{1}h-G_{0}f)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{1}^{2}\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}}+C\varepsilon\|\partial_{1}\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}^{2}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{1}^{2}\mathcal{P}(-c\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{1}h-G_{0}f)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{1}\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{4}h\|_{L^{2}}+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{5}f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon.

For the
L^{4/3}
component, using the decomposition
R_{1}(\eta)\xi=(B_{1}G_{0}+A_{1})\xi+R_{2}(\eta)\xi
and Theorems
3.2
,
3.4
,
3.8
:

\displaystyle\|B_{1}G_{0}\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}+\|A_{1}\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{H^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}+\|\eta\|_{W^{3,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}^{2}\xi\|_{H^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{11/4},

\displaystyle\|R_{2}(\eta)\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\eta\|_{C^{4}})\|\eta\|_{C^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\eta\|_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\xi\|_{W^{4,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}+\|\eta\|_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{{\bf x^{\prime}}}\xi\|_{W^{4,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{15/4}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|\partial_{x_{1}}(-\Delta)R_{1}(\eta)\xi\|_{L^{4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{-7/4}\|R_{1}(\eta)\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-7/4}\left(\|B_{1}G_{0}\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}+\|A_{1}\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}+\|R_{2}(\eta)\xi\|_{W^{3,4/3}(\mathbb{R}^{2};{\bf x^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon.

Combining these results gives

\|\partial_{1}\mathcal{P}R_{1}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon.

Applying analogous estimates to the remaining terms yields the desired bounds:

(5.66)

\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon,\quad\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon.

For
\hat{P}_{4}
, we analyze the representative term
\mathcal{P}G_{0}hG_{0}f
to establish the estimates

(5.67)

\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon,\quad\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon.

Using the decomposition
\mathcal{P}G_{0}hG_{0}f=-\partial_{1}^{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f-\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{2}^{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f
, we estimate
\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{**}
and
\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{***}
. For the first component:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{**}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}^{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{4/3}}.

The
L^{2}
-norm of the second derivative satisfies:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}^{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}(hG_{0}f)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\Big(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}h\|_{L^{4}}\|G_{0}f\|_{L^{4}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}f\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}G_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}G_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\Big(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}h\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}f\|_{L^{4}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}f\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{4}f\|_{L^{2}}\Big).

Applying the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality
\|u\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\|u\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}
yields

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-1/4},\quad\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}h\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-1/4},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}f\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-1/4},\quad\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}f\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-1/4}.

Substituting these bounds gives
\|\partial_{1}^{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon
. Similarly,
\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon
.

For the
L^{4/3}
component:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(hG_{0}f)\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\Big(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h\|_{L^{2}}\|G_{0}f\|_{L^{4}}+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}f\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|h\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}G_{0}f\|_{L^{4}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon.

Thus,
\|\partial_{1}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon
. Analogously,
\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}G_{0}hG_{0}f\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon
.

Combining estimates (
5.65
), (
5.66
), (
5.67
) with Proposition
4.2
, we obtain a solution
\mathcal{N}(\phi)
to (
5.64
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}(\phi)\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\|\partial_{1}^{-1}\hat{P}_{1}\|_{**}+\|\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}\|_{***}+\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{**}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}\|_{***}+\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}\|_{**}+\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}\|_{***}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon.

Consequently, the map
\phi\mapsto\mathcal{N}(\phi)
sends
\mathcal{F}_{\phi}
to itself when
C
is sufficiently large.

Next, we prove that the map
\phi\mapsto\mathcal{N}(\phi)
is a contraction. Specifically, for any
\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\in\mathcal{F}_{\phi}
,

(5.68)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}^{-1}\hat{P}_{1}(\phi_{1})-\partial_{1}^{-1}\hat{P}_{1}(\phi_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}(\phi_{1})-\partial_{2}^{-1}\hat{P}_{2}(\phi_{2})\|_{***}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}(\phi_{1})-\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}(\phi_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}(\phi_{1})-\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}P_{3}(\phi_{2})\|_{***}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}(\phi_{1})-\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}(\phi_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}(\phi_{1})-\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}\hat{P}_{4}(\phi_{2})\|_{***}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

We establish these estimates by analyzing representative terms from
\hat{P}_{1}
,
\hat{P}_{2}
,
P_{3}
, and
\hat{P}_{4}
. To emphasize the dependence on
\phi
, we denote
h=h_{\phi}
and
f=f_{\phi}
.

Term from
\hat{P}_{1}
:
\partial_{1}((\partial_{1}\phi)^{2})
.
We analyze the term
\partial_{1}((\partial_{1}\phi)^{2})
. Direct computation yields the following estimates:

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{1}\phi_{1})^{2}-(\partial_{1}\phi_{2})^{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}+\phi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{1}\phi_{1})^{2}-(\partial_{1}\phi_{2})^{2}\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}+\phi_{2})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}((\partial_{1}\phi_{1})^{2})-\partial_{1}((\partial_{1}\phi_{2})^{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{1}^{2}(\phi_{1}+\phi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{1}(\phi_{1}+\phi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{1}^{2}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Combining these estimates gives

\|(\partial_{1}\phi_{1})^{2}-(\partial_{1}\phi_{2})^{2}\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Term from
\hat{P}_{2}
:
\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{2}(h\partial_{2}f)
.
By Lemmas
5.2
and
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{1}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\|(h_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}})\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}(f_{\phi_{1}}-f_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{2}(q+\phi_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{1}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\|(h_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}})\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}(f_{\phi_{1}}-f_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{4/3}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}(h_{\phi_{1}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}})-\partial_{2}(h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}[(h_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}})\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}]\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}[h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}(f_{\phi_{1}}-f_{\phi_{2}})]\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}(h_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{2}^{2}f_{\phi_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{2}h_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}(f_{\phi_{1}}-f_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}^{2}(f_{\phi_{1}}-f_{\phi_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Thus, combining the components of the
\|\cdot\|_{***}
norm:

\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{\phi_{1}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{1}}-h_{\phi_{2}}\partial_{2}f_{\phi_{2}}\|_{***}\leq C\varepsilon^{1/2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Term from
P_{3}
:
\varepsilon^{-4}R_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f
.
Consider the decomposition:

\varepsilon^{-4}{R}_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h)f=-\varepsilon^{-4}\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta)\xi-\varepsilon^{-3}\partial_{2}\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta)\xi.

For the first term
\varepsilon^{-4}\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta)\xi
, Theorem
3.5
and Corollary
3.4
imply:

\displaystyle\|\varepsilon^{-4}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\||D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\Big(\||D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\big)\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\||D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\Big(\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\||D|^{-1}\tfrac{d}{dt}{R}_{3}(\eta_{t})\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\||D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{4}}\|\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{11}{4}}\|\xi_{2}-\xi_{1}\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}\|\psi_{2}-\psi_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{2}-\psi_{1})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C\varepsilon^{3}\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\phi_{2}-\phi_{1})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}\|\psi_{2}-\psi_{1}\|_{h}+C\varepsilon^{3}\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{*},

where
\eta_{t}=\eta_{1}+t(\eta_{2}-\eta_{1})
for
t\in[0,1]
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\|\varepsilon^{-4}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2}\big)\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\||D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2}\big)\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\Big(\||D|^{-1}\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\big)\xi_{1}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\||D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\Big(\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\||D|^{-1}\tfrac{d}{dt}{R}_{3}(\eta_{t})\xi_{1}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\||D|^{-1}{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{11}{4}}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{W^{1,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}\|\psi_{2}-\psi_{1}\|_{h}+C\varepsilon^{3}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

By Corollary
3.5
and Theorem
3.6
:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}(R_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-R_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{2}}\|{R}_{3}(\eta_{1})\xi_{1}-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\xi_{2}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{2}}\Big(\|\big({R}_{3}(\eta_{1})-{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})\big)\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+\|{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{2}}\Big(\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\|\tfrac{d}{dt}{R}_{3}(\eta_{t})\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+\|{R}_{3}(\eta_{2})(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\left(\|\eta_{2}-\eta_{1}\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{W^{2,4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\big(\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+C\varepsilon\big(\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{*}.

Therefore,

\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)|D|^{-1}\left({R}_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h_{\phi_{1}})f_{\phi_{1}}-{R}_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h_{\phi_{2}})f_{\phi_{2}}\right)\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{*}.

Similarly,

\varepsilon^{-3}\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)|D|^{-1}\left({R}_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h_{\phi_{1}})f_{\phi_{1}}-{R}_{3}(\varepsilon^{2}h_{\phi_{2}})f_{\phi_{2}}\right)\|_{**}\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{2}-\phi_{1}\|_{*}.

Term from
\hat{P}_{4}
:
\varepsilon^{-2}(G_{0}hG_{0}f)
.
We decompose the expression as follows:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-2}(G_{0}hG_{0}f)

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-5}G_{0}(\eta G_{0}\xi)

\displaystyle=-\varepsilon^{-5}\partial_{x_{1}}\Bigl[\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh(|D|)(\eta G_{0}\xi)\Bigr]-\varepsilon^{-5}\partial_{x_{2}}\Bigl[\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)\tanh(|D|)(\eta G_{0}\xi)\Bigr]

\displaystyle=-\varepsilon^{-4}\partial_{1}\Bigl[\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh(|D|)(\eta G_{0}\xi)\Bigr]-\varepsilon^{-3}\partial_{2}\Bigl[\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)\tanh(|D|)(\eta G_{0}\xi)\Bigr].

From Remark
3.1
and Lemmas
5.2
,
5.3
:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\big(\|(\eta_{1}-\eta_{2})G_{0}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\eta_{2}G_{0}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\big(\|\eta_{1}-\eta_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|G_{0}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\eta_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|G_{0}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{5}{2}}\big(\|\eta_{1}-\eta_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{2}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\eta_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{2}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

where we used the inequality:

\|G_{0}\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}=\||\mathbf{k}|\tanh(|\mathbf{k}|)\widehat{\xi}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\leq C\||\mathbf{k}|^{2}\widehat{\xi}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\leq C\|\partial_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{2}\xi\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}.

For the
L^{4/3}
norm:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\big(\|(\eta_{1}-\eta_{2})G_{0}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}+\|\eta_{2}G_{0}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\big(\|\eta_{1}-\eta_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|G_{0}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\eta_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|G_{0}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{7}{4}}\big(\|\eta_{1}-\eta_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{2}\xi_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\|\eta_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\|\partial_{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}^{2}(\xi_{1}-\xi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2};\mathbf{x^{\prime}})}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}+C\varepsilon\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}\left(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-1}\|\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}\left(h_{1}G_{0}f_{1}-h_{2}G_{0}f_{2}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\partial_{1}\tanh{(|D|)}\left(h_{1}G_{0}f_{1}-h_{2}G_{0}f_{2}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\partial_{1}|D|\left(h_{1}G_{0}f_{1}-h_{2}G_{0}f_{2}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|G_{0}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\|h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}G_{0}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{2})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}\Big).

By the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality:

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h},

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{2}G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{1/2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{4}}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{4}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{1/2}\|\nabla_{\varepsilon}^{3}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}^{1/2}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{4}},

\displaystyle\|G_{0}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{4}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\partial_{1}\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}\left(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2}\right)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|_{h}+\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Analogous estimates hold for
\varepsilon^{-3}\partial_{2}\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)\tanh(|D|)(\eta G_{0}\xi)
, yielding:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-4}\|\mathscr{R}_{1}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-3}\|\mathscr{R}_{2}(D)\tanh{(|D|)}(\eta_{1}G_{0}\xi_{1}-\eta_{2}G_{0}\xi_{2})\|_{**}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

Conclusion

The remaining terms in
\hat{P}_{1}
,
\hat{P}_{2}
,
P_{3}
, and
\hat{P}_{4}
admit similar estimates. Applying (
5.68
) and Proposition
4.2
:

\left\|\mathcal{N}(\phi_{1})-\mathcal{N}(\phi_{2})\right\|_{*}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|_{*}.

The existence of a solution
\phi
to (
5.64
) follows from the contraction mapping principle, which establishes the main theorem.
∎
